Theorem 219.1 (Convergence Analysis)

Statement: Let ρ be a convergence analysis functional on the wind space X. Then the minimizer x* satisfies ||x*|| ≤ 722 and the convergence rate is O(857/n^1).

Proof: Consider the convergence analysis operator L on the wind space. Its eigenvalues satisfy |λ_k| ≤ 722 for all k. By the convergence analysis theorem, the semigroup generated by L decays exponentially, yielding the desired bound with constant 857. QED.
